  162|      0|			static_cast<uint64_t>(cutoffTime) * 100000ull;

 1607|       |
 1608|       |	// Presence and boundary probes must use replay-owned buckets. The query
 1609|       |	// caches back borrowed WTSTick/Level2 slices, and changing their date here
 1610|       |	// would invalidate those slices before the caller has released them.
 1611|      2|	collectBoundaries(_tick_sub_map, _replay_ticks_cache);
 1612|      2|	collectBoundaries(_orddtl_sub_map, _replay_orddtl_cache);
 1613|      2|	collectBoundaries(_ordque_sub_map, _replay_ordque_cache);
 1614|      2|	collectBoundaries(_trans_sub_map, _replay_trans_cache);
 1615|      2|	if (!validBoundaries)
 1616|      0|		return false;
 1617|      2|	const uint64_t currentTime = static_cast<uint64_t>(_cur_date) * 1000000000 +
 1618|      2|		static_cast<uint64_t>(_cur_time) * 100000 + _cur_secs;
 1619|      2|	if (tradingDate == firstTradingDate || !hasReplayableEvents)
 1620|      2|	{
 1621|      2|		if (!hasReplayableEvents)
 1622|      2|		{
 1623|      2|			naturalTime = std::max(_tick_begin_time, currentTime);
 1624|      2|		}
 1625|      0|		else
 1626|      0|			naturalTime = std::max(_tick_begin_time, currentTime);
 1627|      2|		return true;
 1628|      2|	}
 1629|      0|	if (earliestBoundary == std::numeric_limits<uint64_t>::max())
 1630|      0|	{
 1631|      0|		WTSLogger::error("Cannot determine session-begin clock for trading date {}: no boundary for replayable HFT data",
 1632|      0|			tradingDate);
 1633|      0|		return false;
 1634|      0|	}
 1635|       |	// Session callbacks share one observable clock across products. A session
 1636|       |	// boundary from a later trading date can map to an earlier natural date for
 1637|       |	// an overnight instrument; never move the public clock backwards.
 1638|      0|	naturalTime = std::max(std::max(earliestBoundary, _tick_begin_time), currentTime);
 1639|      0|	return true;

 1642|       |template<typename T>
 1643|       |void HisDataReplayer::clamp_hft_cursor(HftDataList<T>& itemList)
 1644|      0|{
 1645|      0|	if (!_time_range_valid || itemList._cursor == 0 || itemList._cursor == UINT_MAX)
 1646|      0|		return;
 1647|      0|
 1648|      0|	const std::size_t validCount = std::min(itemList._count, itemList._items.size());
 1649|      0|	while (itemList._cursor <= validCount)
 1650|      0|	{
 1651|      0|		const T& item = itemList._items[itemList._cursor - 1];
 1652|      0|		const uint64_t itemTime = static_cast<uint64_t>(item.action_date) * 1000000000 + item.action_time;
 1653|      0|		if (itemTime < _tick_begin_time)
 1654|      0|		{
 1655|      0|			++itemList._cursor;
 1656|      0|			continue;
 1657|      0|		}
 1658|      0|		if (itemTime >= _tick_end_exclusive_time)
 1659|      0|			itemList._cursor = validCount + 1;

 3355|      6|	auto failedReadIsFatal = [this, tDate, &stdCode](uint32_t kind)
 3356|      6|	{

 3358|       |		// decode as false. For local bin/DSB sources, inspect the exact candidate
 3359|       |		// paths after the read: absent with no filesystem error is a missing day;

 3840|      3|	struct ReplayEvent { uint64_t time; std::size_t round; uint32_t kind; std::string code; uint32_t tDate; std::size_t index; void* list; };
 3841|      3|	struct LaterEvent
 3842|      3|	{
 3843|      3|		bool operator()(const ReplayEvent& left, const ReplayEvent& right) const
 3844|      3|		{
 3845|      0|			if (left.time != right.time) return left.time > right.time;
 3846|       |			// Preserve the legacy same-millisecond rounds: the first record from
 3847|       |			// every D/T/K/Q stream is delivered before any stream's second record.
 3848|      0|			if (left.round != right.round) return left.round > right.round;
 3849|      0|			if (left.kind != right.kind) return left.kind > right.kind;
 3850|      0|			if (left.code != right.code) return left.code > right.code;
 3851|      0|			if (left.tDate != right.tDate) return left.tDate > right.tDate;
 3852|      0|			return left.index > right.index;
 3853|      0|		}
 3854|      3|	};

 6158|       |bool HisDataReplayer::cacheRawTicksFromLoader(HftDataList<WTSTickStruct>& dataList, const char* stdCode, uint32_t uDate)
 6159|      3|{
 6160|      3|	if (NULL == _bt_loader)
 6161|      0|		return false;
 6162|       |
 6163|      3|	dataList._cursor = UINT_MAX;
 6164|      3|	dataList._query_cursor = UINT_MAX;
 6165|      3|	dataList._code = stdCode;
 6166|      3|	dataList._date = uDate;
 6167|      3|	dataList._count = 0;
 6168|       |
 6169|      3|	struct TickLoaderContext
 6170|      3|	{
 6171|      3|		std::mutex mutex;
 6172|      3|		std::vector<WTSTickStruct> items;
 6173|      3|		bool valid { true };
 6174|      3|	};
 6175|      3|	TickLoaderContext context;
 6176|       |
 6177|       |	// FuncReadTicks is a C function pointer, so the thunk must not capture.
 6178|       |	// IBtDataLoader requires every callback to finish before returning. Collect
 6179|       |	// owned copies under a per-call lock so ordered batches from different
 6180|       |	// callback threads cannot race or retain caller-owned memory.
 6181|       |	// The loader must return obj unchanged; an arbitrary wrong pointer cannot be
 6182|       |	// safely inspected by a C thunk and is a loader-contract violation.
 6183|      3|	bool bSucc = _bt_loader->loadRawHisTicks(&context, stdCode, uDate,
 6184|      3|		[](void* obj, WTSTickStruct* firstItem, uint32_t count) {
